# Session 3098, line 166
import numpy as np
from joblib import Parallel, delayed
from astropy import units as u, constants as const


def run_parallel(function_parameters):
    """
    Parallel-compute heating-rate diagnostics across user-defined bins.

    External deps expected in scope: func, turb, ampl_funcs, u, const.
    """

    # ── unpack essentials ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    df          = function_parameters["merged_df"]
    bin_edges   = np.asarray(function_parameters["bin_edges"])
    binned_by   = function_parameters["binned_by"]
    percentiles = np.asarray(function_parameters["percentiles"])

    which_Tp    = function_parameters["which_Tp"]
    which_Te    = function_parameters["which_Te"]
    n_fit_bins  = function_parameters["n_fit_bins"]
    nbins       = function_parameters["nbins"]
    what        = function_parameters["what"]
    units       = function_parameters["units"]
    n_jobs      = function_parameters["n_jobs"]

    AU_TO_R_SUN = function_parameters["AU_TO_R_SUN"]
    AU_IN_KM    = function_parameters.get("AU_IN_KM", 149_597_870.7)
    R_units     = function_parameters.get("R_units", "AU")

    R_min_user  = function_parameters["R_min"]
    R_max_user  = function_parameters["R_max"]
    if R_units.lower() in {"rsun", "rs", "r_sun", "r⊙"}:
        R_min_au, R_max_au = R_min_user / AU_TO_R_SUN, R_max_user / AU_TO_R_SUN
        R_min_rsun, R_max_rsun = R_min_user, R_max_user
    else:
        R_min_au, R_max_au = R_min_user, R_max_user
        R_min_rsun, R_max_rsun = R_min_au * AU_TO_R_SUN, R_max_au * AU_TO_R_SUN

    max_Ma_r    = function_parameters["max_Ma_r"]
    min_Ma_r    = function_parameters["min_Ma_r"]
    fa_fk_max   = function_parameters["fa_fk_max_ratio"]
    mf_min_pct  = function_parameters["mf_min_percentile"]
    model       = function_parameters["model"]

    which_AW_flux = function_parameters["which_AW_flux"]
    use_perp      = function_parameters.get("use_perp", "")

    # Allow both CH09/Cr09 and the common O/0 typos.
    def _get_pol(section, key):
        pol = function_parameters["pol_order"]
        for s in {section, section.replace("0", "O"), section.replace("O", "0")}:
            if s in pol and key in pol[s]:
                return pol[s][key]
        raise KeyError(f"pol_order['{section}']['{key}'] not found (tried O/0 variants).")

    deg_CH09_Q  = _get_pol("CH09", "Q")
    deg_Cr09_Tp = _get_pol("Cr09", "Tp")
    deg_Cr09_Te = _get_pol("Cr09", "Te")
    deg_Cr09_Np = _get_pol("Cr09", "Np")
    deg_Cr09_qe = _get_pol("Cr09", "qe")
    deg_Cr09_Phi= _get_pol("Cr09", "Phi")

    # ── numeric arrays (no unit-ed Series) ─────────────────────────────
    d_au   = df["d"].to_numpy()
    Vsw    = df["Vsw"].to_numpy()
    rho    = df["rho"].to_numpy()      # kg/m^3 downstream
    Ma_r   = df[what_Ma].to_numpy()
    Va     = df["Va"].to_numpy()
    Zp_amp = df[f"Zp_amp{use_perp}"].to_numpy()
    Zm_amp = df[f"Zm_amp{use_perp}"].to_numpy()

    # ── mass flux proxy & stable cuts ───────────────────────────────────
    mf_data = Vsw * (rho * 1e9) * ((d_au * AU_IN_KM) ** 2)
    mf_cut_lo = np.percentile(mf_data[np.isfinite(mf_data)], mf_min_pct)
    mf_cut_hi = np.percentile(mf_data[np.isfinite(mf_data)], 99.9)

    # ── Fa/Fk robustly (guard Fk≈0/NaN) ────────────────────────────────
    m_p = const.m_p.value
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        Fa, Fk = ampl_funcs.compute_fluxes(rho / m_p, Zp_amp, Zm_amp, Vsw, Va)
        Fa_over_Fk = Fa / Fk

    # ── data quality masks ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    span_ok = ((df["Vyx_angle"].to_numpy() < function_parameters["Max_Vx_angle"]) 

              & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() < function_parameters["Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"])
              & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() > 1.0 / function_parameters["Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"])
              & (df["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"].to_numpy() > function_parameters["Min_SPAN_SPAN_media_ratio"])


              )

    base_mask = (
        (Ma_r >= min_Ma_r) & (Ma_r <= max_Ma_r)
        & (d_au >= R_min_au) & (d_au <= R_max_au)
        & (Fa_over_Fk < fa_fk_max)
        & (mf_data >= mf_cut_lo) & (mf_data <= mf_cut_hi)
        & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() < 6)
        & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() > 1.0 / 6)
        & (df["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"].to_numpy() > 0.8)
    )


    # ── quantile-edge construction (use same quality gating) ───────────
    quantile_kw = {
        "Nx_bins"       : function_parameters["Nx_bins"],
        "Ny_bins"       : function_parameters["Ny_bins"],
        "log_x"         : function_parameters["log_x"],
        "poly_order"    : function_parameters["poly_order"],
        "auto_poly"     : function_parameters["auto_poly"],
        "max_poly_order": function_parameters["max_poly_order"],
        "model"         : model,
        "n_jobs"        : function_parameters["quantile_n_jobs"],
        "return_counts" : False,
        "return_avg_y"  : False,
        "low_pct"       : function_parameters["quantile_low_pct"],
        "high_pct"      : function_parameters["quantile_high_pct"],
    }

    qmask  = base_mask
    qfuncs = func.compute_quantile_edges_function(
        df["d"][qmask], df["Vsw"][qmask], **quantile_kw
    )

    if model == "parker":
        coefs   = func.build_parker_surrogates(
            qfuncs["fit_parameters"],
            r_min       = qfuncs["x_edges"][0],
            r_max       = qfuncs["x_edges"][-1],
            n_samples   = function_parameters["parker_n_samples"],
            poly_degree = function_parameters["parker_poly_degree"],
        )
        V_edges = func.eval_parker_surrogates(d_au, coefs)  # shape (E, N)
    elif model == "empirical":
        edge_funcs = qfuncs["edge_functions"]
        V_edges = np.vstack([
            (f(d_au) if f is not None else np.full(d_au.shape[0], np.nan, dtype=float))
            for f in edge_funcs
        ])
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown model '{model}'")

    if V_edges.ndim != 2 or V_edges.shape[0] < 2:
        raise RuntimeError("V_edges has invalid shape; need at least two edges (E>=2).")

    E = V_edges.shape[0]
    if len(bin_edges) == E:
        labels = [f"{round(bin_edges[j-1], 2)} – {round(bin_edges[j], 2)}" for j in range(1, E)]
    else:
        labels = [f"Bin {j}/{E-1}" for j in range(1, E)]

    binned_by_vals = df[binned_by].to_numpy()

    # ── per-bin worker ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _process_bin(jj):
        fin = {}

        low, high = V_edges[jj - 1], V_edges[jj]
        with np.errstate(invalid="ignore"):
            mask2 = base_mask & (binned_by_vals > low) & (binned_by_vals <= high)

        if not np.any(mask2):
            fin["label"] = labels[jj - 1]
            return jj - 1, fin

        sub = df.loc[mask2]

        # ---- Fa diagnostics vs R & Ma ---------------------------------
        d_rsun = sub["d"].to_numpy() * AU_TO_R_SUN
        Ma_sel = sub[what_Ma].to_numpy()
        print('Got')
        Fa_sel = Fa[mask2]
        AW_tot = sub[which_AW_flux].to_numpy()

        r_ok    = (d_rsun > 0)
        r_okFa  = r_ok & (Fa_sel > 0)
        r_okAW  = r_ok & (AW_tot > 0)

        fin["Fa_R"]     = func.binned_quantity(d_rsun[r_okFa], Fa_sel[r_okFa], what=what, bins=nbins)
        fin["Fa_tot_R"] = func.binned_quantity(d_rsun[r_okAW], AW_tot[r_okAW], what=what, bins=nbins)

        ma_okFa = (Fa_sel > 0)
        ma_okAW = (AW_tot > 0)

        fin["Fa_Ma"]     = func.binned_quantity(Ma_sel[ma_okFa],  Fa_sel[ma_okFa],  what=what, bins=nbins)
        fin["Fa_tot_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_sel[ma_okAW],  AW_tot[ma_okAW],  what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- CH09 cascade ---------------------------------------------
        CH09 = turb.CH_09_cascade_rate(
            sub,
            col_Ma   = what_Ma,
            col_r_au = "d",
            col_V0   = "Vsw",
            col_VA0  = "Va",
            col_Zp2  = f"Zp_amp{use_perp}",
            col_rho  = "rho",
            units    = units,
            n_bins   = n_fit_bins,
            poly_deg = deg_CH09_Q,
            R_min    = R_min_au,
            R_max    = R_max_au,
            use_binning = True,
            hetero_fit  = "ols",
            quant_is_squared = True,
        )
        fin["CH09_fits"] = CH09["fits"]

        x_CH09_au = np.asarray(CH09["x"])
        y_CH09    = np.asarray(CH09["y"])
        Ma_CH09   = np.asarray(CH09['Ma'])
        sh_CH09   = np.abs(np.asarray(CH09["scale_height"]))

        r_okQ   = (x_CH09_au > 0) & (y_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(x_CH09_au) & np.isfinite(y_CH09)
        ma_okQ  = (Ma_CH09 > 0) & (Ma_CH09 <= max_Ma_r) & (y_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_CH09) & np.isfinite(y_CH09)
        r_okSH  = (x_CH09_au > 0) & (sh_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(x_CH09_au) & np.isfinite(sh_CH09)
        ma_okSH = (Ma_CH09 > 0) & (Ma_CH09 <= max_Ma_r) & (sh_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_CH09) & np.isfinite(sh_CH09)

        fin["CH09_R"]               = func.binned_quantity(x_CH09_au[r_okQ] * AU_TO_R_SUN, y_CH09[r_okQ], what=what, bins=nbins)
        fin["CH09_Ma"]              = func.binned_quantity(Ma_CH09[ma_okQ],                y_CH09[ma_okQ], what=what, bins=nbins)
        fin["CH09_scale_height_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(x_CH09_au[r_okSH] * AU_TO_R_SUN, sh_CH09[r_okSH], what=what, bins=nbins)
        fin["CH09_scale_height_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_CH09[ma_okSH],               sh_CH09[ma_okSH], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- Cr09 cascade (ONLY mask Tp in-place; keep rows/order) -----
        idx_span_sub = span_ok[mask2]

        # backup Tp, set NaN only where span fails, call, then restore

        sub.loc[~idx_span_sub, which_Tp] = np.nan

        Cr09_dict, fits = turb.Cr09_cascade_rate(
            sub,
            deg_Tp  = deg_Cr09_Tp,
            deg_Te  = deg_Cr09_Te,
            deg_n   = deg_Cr09_Np,
            deg_q   = deg_Cr09_qe,
            deg_phi = deg_Cr09_Phi,
            n_bins  = n_fit_bins,
            R_min   = R_min_au,
            R_max   = R_max_au,
            which_Te= which_Te,
            which_Tp= which_Tp,
        )


        fin["Cr09_fits"] = fits

        d_cr_rsun = Cr09_dict["d"].to_numpy() * AU_TO_R_SUN
        Ma_cr     = Cr09_dict['Ma'].to_numpy()

        for Q in ("Qe", "Qp", "Qe_qpar"):
            y = Cr09_dict[Q].to_numpy()
            r_ok = (d_cr_rsun > 0) & (y > 0) & np.isfinite(d_cr_rsun) & np.isfinite(y)
            m_ok = (Ma_cr > 0) & (Ma_cr <= max_Ma_r) & (y > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_cr) & np.isfinite(y)
            fin[f"Cr09_{Q}_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(d_cr_rsun[r_ok], y[r_ok], what=what, bins=nbins)
            fin[f"Cr09_{Q}_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_cr[m_ok],    y[m_ok], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- PP98 (SPAN-quality only, same mask) -----------------------
        if np.any(idx_span_sub):
            xv = AU_TO_R_SUN * sub["d"].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub]
            yv = 0.5 * (4.0 / 3.0) * sub["QPP98"].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub]
            fin["PP98_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(xv, yv, what=what, bins=nbins)
            fin["PP98_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(sub[what_Ma].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub], yv, what=what, bins=nbins)

        fin["label"] = labels[jj - 1]
        print(jj)
        return jj - 1, fin

    # ── parallel execution ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    results = Parallel(n_jobs=n_jobs)(delayed(_process_bin)(jj) for jj in range(1, E))

    out = {"bins": bin_edges}
    for idx, res in results:
        out[idx] = res
    return out



# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Example parameter‑dictionary --------------------------------------------
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

# function_parameters = {

#     "Max_Vx_angle"              : 160,
#     "Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"    : 2,
#     "Min_SPAN_SPAN_media_ratio" : 0.85, 
#     # compulsory data
#     'which_AW_flux'    : which_AW_flux,
#     "merged_df"        : merged_df[mask][::4],
#     "which_Te"         : 'Te_spane',
#     "which_Tp"         : 'Tp',

#     # binning & selection
#     "binned_by"        : "Vsw",
#     "bin_edges"        : [0, 250, 350, 450, 800],
#     "percentiles"      : [0.,0.4,0.4, 1 , 0, 0],

#     "fa_fk_max_ratio"  : 1e2,
#     "mf_min_percentile": 0.3,

#     # modelling options
#     "model"            : "parker",      # {"empirical", "parker"}
#     "n_fit_bins"       :  1000,
#     "statistic"        : "mean",
#     "units"            : "SI",

#     # Parker‑surrogate hyper‑parameters
#     "parker_n_samples"  : 1000,
#     "parker_poly_degree": 10,

#     # quantile‑grid hyper‑parameters
#     "Nx_bins"          : 60,
#     "Ny_bins"          : 4,
#     "log_x"            : True,
#     "poly_order"       : None,
#     "auto_poly"        : True,
#     "max_poly_order"   : 4,
#     "quantile_n_jobs"  : 1,
#     "quantile_low_pct" : 0.01,
#     "quantile_high_pct": 97.0,

#     # miscellaneous numerics
#     "nbins"            : 120,
#     "what"             : "mean",
#     "n_jobs"           : 4,     # joblib cores
#     "R_min"            : 9/215.032,
#     "R_max"            : 45/215.032,
#     "max_Ma_r"         : 30,
#     "min_Ma_r"         : 0.3,

#     # constants
#     "AU_TO_R_SUN"      : 215.032,   
#     "R_units"          : 'AU',



#     # Polynomial order
#     'pol_order'       : {
#                                 'CHO9': {  'Q'  : 1},

#                                 'CrO9': { 'Tp'  : 2, 
#                                           'Te'  : 2,
#                                           'Np'  : 2,
#                                           'Ne'  : 2,
#                                           'qe'  : 2,
#                                           'Phi' : 6} }}

function_parameters = {

    "Max_Vx_angle"              : 160,
    "Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"    : 2.5,
    "Min_SPAN_SPAN_media_ratio" : 0.85, 
    # compulsory data
    'which_AW_flux'    : which_AW_flux,
    "merged_df"        : merged_df[mask][::4],
    "which_Te"         : 'Te_spane',
    "which_Tp"         : 'T_p_Davin',

    # binning & selection
    "binned_by"        : "Vsw",
    "bin_edges"        : [0, 250, 350, 450, 800],
    "percentiles"      : [0.,0.4,0.4, 1 , 0, 0],

for jj in range(length):
    coeff = 20**(jj )




    xv_Ch09, yv_Ch09 = final_results[jj]['CH09_'+x_ax_bin][0], coeff * final_results[jj]['CH09_'+x_ax_bin][1]
    xv_PP98, yv_PP98 = final_results[jj]['PP98_'+x_ax_bin][0], coeff * final_results[jj]['PP98_'+x_ax_bin][1]#/((1/2)*(4/3))
    xv_Cr09, yv_Cr09 = final_results[jj]['Cr09_Qp_'+x_ax_bin][0], coeff * (final_results[jj]['Cr09_Qp_'+x_ax_bin][1])
    label = final_results[jj]['label'] 

    print(max(xv_Cr09))

    if jj == 0:
        plt.semilogy(xv_PP98, abs(yv_PP98), label='PP98', alpha=0.5, c=c[jj])
        plt.loglog(xv_Cr09, yv_Cr09, label='Qp', lw=2, alpha=0.9, c=c[jj], ls=':')
        plt.loglog(xv_Ch09, yv_Ch09, label='CH09', lw=2, alpha=0.9, c=c[jj])
    else:
        plt.semilogy(xv_PP98, abs(yv_PP98), alpha=0.5, c=c[jj])
        plt.loglog(xv_Cr09, yv_Cr09, lw=2, alpha=0.9, c=c[jj], ls=':')
        plt.loglog(xv_Ch09, yv_Ch09, lw=2, alpha=0.9, c=c[jj])

    # Cr09_Qp + Cr09_Qe
    xv_Cr09, yv_Cr09 = final_results[jj]['Cr09_Qp_'+x_ax_bin][0], coeff * (
        final_results[jj]['Cr09_Qp_'+x_ax_bin][1] + final_results[jj]['Cr09_Qe_'+x_ax_bin][1]
    )
    if jj == 0:
        plt.loglog(xv_Cr09, yv_Cr09, label='Cr09_Qe', lw=2, alpha=0.9, c=c[jj], ls='-.')
    else:
        plt.loglog(xv_Cr09, yv_Cr09, lw=2, alpha=0.9, c=c[jj], ls='-.')

    # Cr09_Qp + Cr09_Qe_qpar
    xv_Cr09, yv_Cr09 = final_results[jj]['Cr09_Qp_'+x_ax_bin][0], coeff * (
        final_results[jj]['Cr09_Qp_'+x_ax_bin][1] + final_results[jj]['Cr09_Qe_qpar_'+x_ax_bin][1]
    )
    if jj == 0:
        plt.loglog(xv_Cr09, yv_Cr09, label='Cr09_Qe_qpar', lw=3, alpha=0.3, c=c[jj], ls='-.')
    else:
        plt.loglog(xv_Cr09, yv_Cr09, lw=3, alpha=0.3, c=c[jj], ls='-.')

# Session 3100, line 2
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from joblib import Parallel, delayed
from astropy import units as u, constants as const

COLUMNS = ["datetime", "Dist_au", "Tp", "Vr", "Vt", "Vn", "Vsw", "np", "rho"]

def _extract_one(path):
    try:
        obj = pd.read_pickle(path)

        par = obj.get("Par", None) if isinstance(obj, dict) else getattr(obj, "Par", None)
        if par is None:
            return None

        vres = par.get("V_resampled", None) if isinstance(par, dict) else par["V_resampled"]
        if not isinstance(vres, pd.DataFrame):
            return None

        needed = {"Dist_au", "Tp", "Vr", "Vt", "Vn", "np"}
        if not needed.issubset(vres.columns):
            return None

        v = vres[["Vr", "Vt", "Vn"]].to_numpy()
        vsw = np.sqrt((v * v).sum(axis=1))

        np_cm3 = vres["np"].to_numpy()
        rho = (np_cm3 * (1 / u.cm**3) * const.m_p).to(u.kg / u.cm**3).value

        out = pd.DataFrame(
            {
                "datetime": vres.index,
                "d"       : vres["Dist_au"].to_numpy(),
                "Tp"      :      vres["Tp"].to_numpy(),
                "T_p_Davin"      :      vres["Tp"].to_numpy(),
                "Vr"      :      v[:, 0],
                "Vt"      :      v[:, 1],
                "Vn"      :      v[:, 2],
                "Vsw"     :     vsw,
                "np"      :      np_cm3,
                "rho"     :     rho,
            }
        )
        return out

    except Exception:
        return None


sc        = 'SOLO'
dur, step = '24H', '20H'
load_path = f'/Volumes/shoezoo/nsioulas/turb_amplitudes/{sc}/{dur}_{step}'
fin       = func.load_files(load_path, 'final.pkl', '')

frames = Parallel(n_jobs=-1, prefer="threads")(delayed(_extract_one)(p) for p in fin)
final_df2 = pd.concat([df for df in frames if df is not None and not df.empty], ignore_index=True)

#bi =   func.binned_quantity(215.032*final_df['Dist_au'].values, final_df['Tp'].values)

#plt.loglog(bi[0], bi[1])

# Session 3100, line 4
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from joblib import Parallel, delayed
from astropy import units as u, constants as const

COLUMNS = ["datetime", "Dist_au", "Tp", "Vr", "Vt", "Vn", "Vsw", "np", "rho"]

def _extract_one(path):
    try:
        obj = pd.read_pickle(path)

        par = obj.get("Par", None) if isinstance(obj, dict) else getattr(obj, "Par", None)
        if par is None:
            return None

        vres = par.get("V_resampled", None) if isinstance(par, dict) else par["V_resampled"]
        if not isinstance(vres, pd.DataFrame):
            return None

        needed = {"Dist_au", "Tp", "Vr", "Vt", "Vn", "np"}
        if not needed.issubset(vres.columns):
            return None

        v = vres[["Vr", "Vt", "Vn"]].to_numpy()
        vsw = np.sqrt((v * v).sum(axis=1))

        np_cm3 = vres["np"].to_numpy()
        rho = (np_cm3 * (1 / u.cm**3) * const.m_p).to(u.kg / u.cm**3).value

        out = pd.DataFrame(
            {
                "datetime": vres.index,
                "d"       : vres["Dist_au"].to_numpy(),
                "Tp"      :      vres["Tp"].to_numpy(),
                "T_p_Davin"      :      vres["Tp"].to_numpy(),
                "Vr"      :      v[:, 0],
                "Vt"      :      v[:, 1],
                "Vn"      :      v[:, 2],
                "Vsw"     :     vsw,
                "np"      :      np_cm3,
                "rho"     :     rho,
            }
        )
        return out

    except Exception:
        return None

final_df2 = pd.concat([df for df in frames if df is not None and not df.empty], ignore_index=True)

# Session 3100, line 7
use_perp      = ''
which_Te      = 'Te_spane'
what_Ma       = 'Ma'
quants_2_load = [ 'Np', 'Ne', 'Phi',  'rho', 'Tp', 'beta', 'V0', 'Vsw', what_Ma, 'Va',  'Vr',  'Zm_amp'+use_perp, 'Zp_amp'+use_perp, 'compress_simple', 'compress_simple_V', 'sig_c', 'sig_r', 'd']

use_heat_flux      = True
sc                 = 'PSP'
wind_di            =  4096
use_npt            = '5pt'
av_window          = '10min'
dur_PSP, step_PSP  = 8, 4
dur_SO, step_SO    = 24, 20
# Load dataframes


# Load PSP data
extras          = [which_Te, 'T_p_Davin',  'Vyx_angle',  "SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio", 'SPAN_QTN_ratio', 'qpar' ]
psp_data        = func.load_parquet(f'/Users/turbulator/work/turb_amplitudes/data/PSP/increments/Monc_{dur_PSP}H_{step_PSP}H/_heat_flux/_window_{av_window}/_di_{wind_di}_correct_sign.parquet',    column_names  = quants_2_load +extras)


psp_conds       = (
                         (psp_data['d'] * 215.032 < 100)
                       & ~((psp_data['beta'] > 3) & (psp_data[what_Ma] < 1)) &
                       & (psp_data['compress_simple'] >1e-7)
                       & (psp_data['compress_simple_V'] >1e-7)
                       & (psp_data['compress_simple'] <2)
                       & (psp_data['compress_simple_V'] <2)
                       & (psp_data['sig_r'] <0.3)
                       & (np.abs(psp_data['sig_c']) >0.02)
                       & (np.abs(psp_data["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"]) > 0.85)
                       & (np.abs(psp_data['SPAN_QTN_ratio']) > 1/6)
                       & (np.abs(psp_data['SPAN_QTN_ratio']) < 6)
                  )

psp_data        = psp_data.loc[psp_conds]

del psp_conds, psp_data['beta'], psp_data['compress_simple'], psp_data['compress_simple_V'], psp_data['sig_c'], psp_data['sig_r'],  psp_data["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"],  psp_data["Vyx_angle"],  psp_data["SPAN_QTN_ratio"],  psp_data["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"]



#Load SOLO data
solo_data        = func.load_parquet(f'/Users/turbulator/work/turb_amplitudes/data/PSP/increments/Monc_{dur_SO}_H_{step_SO}H/_heat_flux/_window_{av_window}/_di_{wind_di}_correct_sign.parquet',    column_names  = quants_2_load )


solo_conds       = (
                         (solo_data['compress_simple'] >1e-7)
                       & (solo_data['compress_simple_V'] >1e-7)
                       & (solo_data['compress_simple'] <2)
                       & (solo_data['compress_simple_V'] <2)
                       & (solo_data['sig_r'] <0.3)
                       & (np.abs(solo_data['sig_c']) >0.02)
                       & ~((solo_data['rho'] / m_p > 2e2) | (solo_data['rho'] / m_p < 1e0) |  (solo_data[what_Ma] <1))
                  )

del solo_conds, solo_data['compress_simple'], solo_data['compress_simple_V'], solo_data['sig_c'], solo_data['sig_r']



# merged_df     = pd.concat([solo_data, solo_data])
# del psp_data, solo_data

# Session 3100, line 28
import numpy as np
from joblib import Parallel, delayed
from astropy import units as u, constants as const


def run_parallel(function_parameters):
    """
    Parallel-compute heating-rate diagnostics across user-defined bins.

    External deps expected in scope: func, turb, ampl_funcs, u, const.
    """

    # ── unpack essentials ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    df          = function_parameters["merged_df"]
    bin_edges   = np.asarray(function_parameters["bin_edges"])
    binned_by   = function_parameters["binned_by"]
    percentiles = np.asarray(function_parameters["percentiles"])

    which_Tp    = function_parameters["which_Tp"]
    which_Te    = function_parameters["which_Te"]
    n_fit_bins  = function_parameters["n_fit_bins"]
    nbins       = function_parameters["nbins"]
    what        = function_parameters["what"]
    units       = function_parameters["units"]
    n_jobs      = function_parameters["n_jobs"]

    AU_TO_R_SUN = function_parameters["AU_TO_R_SUN"]
    AU_IN_KM    = function_parameters.get("AU_IN_KM", 149_597_870.7)
    R_units     = function_parameters.get("R_units", "AU")

    R_min_user  = function_parameters["R_min"]
    R_max_user  = function_parameters["R_max"]
    if R_units.lower() in {"rsun", "rs", "r_sun", "r⊙"}:
        R_min_au, R_max_au = R_min_user / AU_TO_R_SUN, R_max_user / AU_TO_R_SUN
        R_min_rsun, R_max_rsun = R_min_user, R_max_user
    else:
        R_min_au, R_max_au = R_min_user, R_max_user
        R_min_rsun, R_max_rsun = R_min_au * AU_TO_R_SUN, R_max_au * AU_TO_R_SUN

    max_Ma_r    = function_parameters["max_Ma_r"]
    min_Ma_r    = function_parameters["min_Ma_r"]
    fa_fk_max   = function_parameters["fa_fk_max_ratio"]
    mf_min_pct  = function_parameters["mf_min_percentile"]
    model       = function_parameters["model"]

    which_AW_flux = function_parameters["which_AW_flux"]
    use_perp      = function_parameters.get("use_perp", "")

    # Allow both CH09/Cr09 and the common O/0 typos.
    def _get_pol(section, key):
        pol = function_parameters["pol_order"]
        for s in {section, section.replace("0", "O"), section.replace("O", "0")}:
            if s in pol and key in pol[s]:
                return pol[s][key]
        raise KeyError(f"pol_order['{section}']['{key}'] not found (tried O/0 variants).")

    deg_CH09_Q  = _get_pol("CH09", "Q")
    deg_Cr09_Tp = _get_pol("Cr09", "Tp")
    deg_Cr09_Te = _get_pol("Cr09", "Te")
    deg_Cr09_Np = _get_pol("Cr09", "Np")
    deg_Cr09_qe = _get_pol("Cr09", "qe")
    deg_Cr09_Phi= _get_pol("Cr09", "Phi")

    # ── numeric arrays (no unit-ed Series) ─────────────────────────────
    d_au   = df["d"].to_numpy()
    Vsw    = df["Vsw"].to_numpy()
    rho    = df["rho"].to_numpy()      # kg/m^3 downstream
    Ma_r   = df[what_Ma].to_numpy()
    Va     = df["Va"].to_numpy()
    Zp_amp = df[f"Zp_amp{use_perp}"].to_numpy()
    Zm_amp = df[f"Zm_amp{use_perp}"].to_numpy()

    # ── mass flux proxy & stable cuts ───────────────────────────────────
    mf_data = Vsw * (rho * 1e9) * ((d_au * AU_IN_KM) ** 2)
    mf_cut_lo = np.percentile(mf_data[np.isfinite(mf_data)], mf_min_pct)
    mf_cut_hi = np.percentile(mf_data[np.isfinite(mf_data)], 99.9)

    # ── Fa/Fk robustly (guard Fk≈0/NaN) ────────────────────────────────
    m_p = const.m_p.value
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        Fa, Fk = ampl_funcs.compute_fluxes(rho / m_p, Zp_amp, Zm_amp, Vsw, Va)
        Fa_over_Fk = Fa / Fk

    # ── data quality masks ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    span_ok = ((df["Vyx_angle"].to_numpy() < function_parameters["Max_Vx_angle"]) 

              & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() < function_parameters["Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"])
              & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() > 1.0 / function_parameters["Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"])
              & (df["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"].to_numpy() > function_parameters["Min_SPAN_SPAN_media_ratio"])


              )

    base_mask = (
        (Ma_r >= min_Ma_r) & (Ma_r <= max_Ma_r)
        & (d_au >= R_min_au) & (d_au <= R_max_au)
        & (Fa_over_Fk < fa_fk_max)
        & (mf_data >= mf_cut_lo) & (mf_data <= mf_cut_hi)
        & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() < 6)
        & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() > 1.0 / 6)
        & (df["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"].to_numpy() > 0.8)
    )


    # ── quantile-edge construction (use same quality gating) ───────────
    quantile_kw = {
        "Nx_bins"       : function_parameters["Nx_bins"],
        "Ny_bins"       : function_parameters["Ny_bins"],
        "log_x"         : function_parameters["log_x"],
        "poly_order"    : function_parameters["poly_order"],
        "auto_poly"     : function_parameters["auto_poly"],
        "max_poly_order": function_parameters["max_poly_order"],
        "model"         : model,
        "n_jobs"        : function_parameters["quantile_n_jobs"],
        "return_counts" : False,
        "return_avg_y"  : False,
        "low_pct"       : function_parameters["quantile_low_pct"],
        "high_pct"      : function_parameters["quantile_high_pct"],
    }

    qmask  = base_mask
    qfuncs = func.compute_quantile_edges_function(
        df["d"][qmask], df["Vsw"][qmask], **quantile_kw
    )

    if model == "parker":
        coefs   = func.build_parker_surrogates(
            qfuncs["fit_parameters"],
            r_min       = qfuncs["x_edges"][0],
            r_max       = qfuncs["x_edges"][-1],
            n_samples   = function_parameters["parker_n_samples"],
            poly_degree = function_parameters["parker_poly_degree"],
        )
        V_edges = func.eval_parker_surrogates(d_au, coefs)  # shape (E, N)
    elif model == "empirical":
        edge_funcs = qfuncs["edge_functions"]
        V_edges = np.vstack([
            (f(d_au) if f is not None else np.full(d_au.shape[0], np.nan, dtype=float))
            for f in edge_funcs
        ])
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown model '{model}'")

    if V_edges.ndim != 2 or V_edges.shape[0] < 2:
        raise RuntimeError("V_edges has invalid shape; need at least two edges (E>=2).")

    E = V_edges.shape[0]
    if len(bin_edges) == E:
        labels = [f"{round(bin_edges[j-1], 2)} – {round(bin_edges[j], 2)}" for j in range(1, E)]
    else:
        labels = [f"Bin {j}/{E-1}" for j in range(1, E)]

    binned_by_vals = df[binned_by].to_numpy()

    # ── per-bin worker ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _process_bin(jj):
        fin = {}

        low, high = V_edges[jj - 1], V_edges[jj]
        with np.errstate(invalid="ignore"):
            mask2 = base_mask & (binned_by_vals > low) & (binned_by_vals <= high)

        if not np.any(mask2):
            fin["label"] = labels[jj - 1]
            return jj - 1, fin

        sub = df.loc[mask2]

        # ---- Fa diagnostics vs R & Ma ---------------------------------
        # d_rsun = sub["d"].to_numpy() * AU_TO_R_SUN
        # Ma_sel = sub[what_Ma].to_numpy()
        # print('Got')
        # Fa_sel = Fa[mask2]
        # AW_tot = sub[which_AW_flux].to_numpy()

        # r_ok    = (d_rsun > 0)
        # r_okFa  = r_ok & (Fa_sel > 0)
        # r_okAW  = r_ok & (AW_tot > 0)

        # fin["Fa_R"]     = func.binned_quantity(d_rsun[r_okFa], Fa_sel[r_okFa], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["Fa_tot_R"] = func.binned_quantity(d_rsun[r_okAW], AW_tot[r_okAW], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ma_okFa = (Fa_sel > 0)
        # ma_okAW = (AW_tot > 0)

        # fin["Fa_Ma"]     = func.binned_quantity(Ma_sel[ma_okFa],  Fa_sel[ma_okFa],  what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["Fa_tot_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_sel[ma_okAW],  AW_tot[ma_okAW],  what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- CH09 cascade ---------------------------------------------
        # CH09 = turb.CH_09_cascade_rate(
        #     sub,
        #     col_Ma   = what_Ma,
        #     col_r_au = "d",
        #     col_V0   = "Vsw",
        #     col_VA0  = "Va",
        #     col_Zp2  = f"Zp_amp{use_perp}",
        #     col_rho  = "rho",
        #     units    = units,
        #     n_bins   = n_fit_bins,
        #     poly_deg = deg_CH09_Q,
        #     R_min    = R_min_au,
        #     R_max    = R_max_au,
        #     use_binning = True,
        #     hetero_fit  = "ols",
        #     quant_is_squared = True,
        # )
        # fin["CH09_fits"] = CH09["fits"]

        # x_CH09_au = np.asarray(CH09["x"])
        # y_CH09    = np.asarray(CH09["y"])
        # Ma_CH09   = np.asarray(CH09['Ma'])
        # sh_CH09   = np.abs(np.asarray(CH09["scale_height"]))

        # r_okQ   = (x_CH09_au > 0) & (y_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(x_CH09_au) & np.isfinite(y_CH09)
        # ma_okQ  = (Ma_CH09 > 0) & (Ma_CH09 <= max_Ma_r) & (y_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_CH09) & np.isfinite(y_CH09)
        # r_okSH  = (x_CH09_au > 0) & (sh_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(x_CH09_au) & np.isfinite(sh_CH09)
        # ma_okSH = (Ma_CH09 > 0) & (Ma_CH09 <= max_Ma_r) & (sh_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_CH09) & np.isfinite(sh_CH09)

        # fin["CH09_R"]               = func.binned_quantity(x_CH09_au[r_okQ] * AU_TO_R_SUN, y_CH09[r_okQ], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_Ma"]              = func.binned_quantity(Ma_CH09[ma_okQ],                y_CH09[ma_okQ], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_scale_height_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(x_CH09_au[r_okSH] * AU_TO_R_SUN, sh_CH09[r_okSH], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_scale_height_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_CH09[ma_okSH],               sh_CH09[ma_okSH], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- Cr09 cascade (ONLY mask Tp in-place; keep rows/order) -----
        idx_span_sub = span_ok[mask2]

        # backup Tp, set NaN only where span fails, call, then restore

        sub.loc[~idx_span_sub, which_Tp] = np.nan

        Cr09_dict, fits = turb.Cr09_cascade_rate(
            sub,
            deg_Tp  = deg_Cr09_Tp,
            deg_Te  = deg_Cr09_Te,
            deg_n   = deg_Cr09_Np,
            deg_q   = deg_Cr09_qe,
            deg_phi = deg_Cr09_Phi,
            n_bins  = n_fit_bins,
            R_min   = R_min_au,
            R_max   = R_max_au,
            which_Te= which_Te,
            which_Tp= which_Tp,
        )


        fin["Cr09_fits"] = fits

        d_cr_rsun = Cr09_dict["d"].to_numpy() * AU_TO_R_SUN
        Ma_cr     = Cr09_dict['Ma'].to_numpy()

        for Q in ("Qe", "Qp", "Qe_qpar"):
            y = Cr09_dict[Q].to_numpy()
            r_ok = (d_cr_rsun > 0) & (y > 0) & np.isfinite(d_cr_rsun) & np.isfinite(y)
            m_ok = (Ma_cr > 0) & (Ma_cr <= max_Ma_r) & (y > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_cr) & np.isfinite(y)
            fin[f"Cr09_{Q}_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(d_cr_rsun[r_ok], y[r_ok], what=what, bins=nbins)
            fin[f"Cr09_{Q}_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_cr[m_ok],    y[m_ok], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- PP98 (SPAN-quality only, same mask) -----------------------
        # if np.any(idx_span_sub):
        #     xv = AU_TO_R_SUN * sub["d"].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub]
        #     yv = 0.5 * (4.0 / 3.0) * sub["QPP98"].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub]
        #     fin["PP98_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(xv, yv, what=what, bins=nbins)
        #     fin["PP98_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(sub[what_Ma].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub], yv, what=what, bins=nbins)

        fin["label"] = labels[jj - 1]
        print(jj)
        return jj - 1, fin

    # ── parallel execution ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    results = Parallel(n_jobs=n_jobs)(delayed(_process_bin)(jj) for jj in range(1, E))

    out = {"bins": bin_edges}
    for idx, res in results:
        out[idx] = res
    return out


function_parameters = {

    "Max_Vx_angle"              : 160,
    "Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"    : 2.5,
    "Min_SPAN_SPAN_media_ratio" : 0.85, 
    # compulsory data
    'which_AW_flux'    : which_AW_flux,
    "merged_df"        : merged_df[mask][::100],
    "which_Te"         : 'Te_spane',
    "which_Tp"         : 'T_p_Davin',

    # binning & selection
    "binned_by"        : "Vsw",
    "bin_edges"        : [0, 250, 350, 450, 800],
    "percentiles"      : [0.,0.4,0.4, 1 , 0, 0],

    "fa_fk_max_ratio"  : 1e2,
    "mf_min_percentile": 0.3,

    # modelling options
    "model"            : "parker",      # {"empirical", "parker"}
    "n_fit_bins"       :  1000,
    "statistic"        : "mean",
    "units"            : "SI",

    # Parker‑surrogate hyper‑parameters
    "parker_n_samples"  : 1000,
    "parker_poly_degree": 10,

    # quantile‑grid hyper‑parameters
    "Nx_bins"          : 60,
    "Ny_bins"          : 4,
    "log_x"            : True,
    "poly_order"       : None,
    "auto_poly"        : True,
    "max_poly_order"   : 4,
    "quantile_n_jobs"  : 1,
    "quantile_low_pct" : 0.01,
    "quantile_high_pct": 97.0,

    # miscellaneous numerics
    "nbins"            : 120,
    "what"             : "mean",
    "n_jobs"           : 4,     # joblib cores
    "R_min"            : 9/215.032,
    "R_max"            : 100/215.032,
    "max_Ma_r"         : 30,
    "min_Ma_r"         : 0.3,

    # constants
    "AU_TO_R_SUN"      : 215.032,

                                'CHO9': {  'Q'  : 2},

# Session 3100, line 29
import numpy as np
from joblib import Parallel, delayed
from astropy import units as u, constants as const


def run_parallel(function_parameters):
    """
    Parallel-compute heating-rate diagnostics across user-defined bins.

    External deps expected in scope: func, turb, ampl_funcs, u, const.
    """

    # ── unpack essentials ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    df          = function_parameters["merged_df"]
    bin_edges   = np.asarray(function_parameters["bin_edges"])
    binned_by   = function_parameters["binned_by"]
    percentiles = np.asarray(function_parameters["percentiles"])

    which_Tp    = function_parameters["which_Tp"]
    which_Te    = function_parameters["which_Te"]
    n_fit_bins  = function_parameters["n_fit_bins"]
    nbins       = function_parameters["nbins"]
    what        = function_parameters["what"]
    units       = function_parameters["units"]
    n_jobs      = function_parameters["n_jobs"]

    AU_TO_R_SUN = function_parameters["AU_TO_R_SUN"]
    AU_IN_KM    = function_parameters.get("AU_IN_KM", 149_597_870.7)
    R_units     = function_parameters.get("R_units", "AU")

    R_min_user  = function_parameters["R_min"]
    R_max_user  = function_parameters["R_max"]
    if R_units.lower() in {"rsun", "rs", "r_sun", "r⊙"}:
        R_min_au, R_max_au = R_min_user / AU_TO_R_SUN, R_max_user / AU_TO_R_SUN
        R_min_rsun, R_max_rsun = R_min_user, R_max_user
    else:
        R_min_au, R_max_au = R_min_user, R_max_user
        R_min_rsun, R_max_rsun = R_min_au * AU_TO_R_SUN, R_max_au * AU_TO_R_SUN

    max_Ma_r    = function_parameters["max_Ma_r"]
    min_Ma_r    = function_parameters["min_Ma_r"]
    fa_fk_max   = function_parameters["fa_fk_max_ratio"]
    mf_min_pct  = function_parameters["mf_min_percentile"]
    model       = function_parameters["model"]

    which_AW_flux = function_parameters["which_AW_flux"]
    use_perp      = function_parameters.get("use_perp", "")

    # Allow both CH09/Cr09 and the common O/0 typos.
    def _get_pol(section, key):
        pol = function_parameters["pol_order"]
        for s in {section, section.replace("0", "O"), section.replace("O", "0")}:
            if s in pol and key in pol[s]:
                return pol[s][key]
        raise KeyError(f"pol_order['{section}']['{key}'] not found (tried O/0 variants).")

    deg_CH09_Q  = _get_pol("CH09", "Q")
    deg_Cr09_Tp = _get_pol("Cr09", "Tp")
    deg_Cr09_Te = _get_pol("Cr09", "Te")
    deg_Cr09_Np = _get_pol("Cr09", "Np")
    deg_Cr09_qe = _get_pol("Cr09", "qe")
    deg_Cr09_Phi= _get_pol("Cr09", "Phi")

    # ── numeric arrays (no unit-ed Series) ─────────────────────────────
    d_au   = df["d"].to_numpy()
    Vsw    = df["Vsw"].to_numpy()
    rho    = df["rho"].to_numpy()      # kg/m^3 downstream
    Ma_r   = df[what_Ma].to_numpy()
    Va     = df["Va"].to_numpy()
    Zp_amp = df[f"Zp_amp{use_perp}"].to_numpy()
    Zm_amp = df[f"Zm_amp{use_perp}"].to_numpy()

    # ── mass flux proxy & stable cuts ───────────────────────────────────
    mf_data = Vsw * (rho * 1e9) * ((d_au * AU_IN_KM) ** 2)
    mf_cut_lo = np.percentile(mf_data[np.isfinite(mf_data)], mf_min_pct)
    mf_cut_hi = np.percentile(mf_data[np.isfinite(mf_data)], 99.9)

    # ── Fa/Fk robustly (guard Fk≈0/NaN) ────────────────────────────────
    m_p = const.m_p.value
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        Fa, Fk = ampl_funcs.compute_fluxes(rho / m_p, Zp_amp, Zm_amp, Vsw, Va)
        Fa_over_Fk = Fa / Fk

    # ── data quality masks ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    span_ok = ((df["Vyx_angle"].to_numpy() < function_parameters["Max_Vx_angle"]) 

              & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() < function_parameters["Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"])
              & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() > 1.0 / function_parameters["Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"])
              & (df["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"].to_numpy() > function_parameters["Min_SPAN_SPAN_media_ratio"])


              )

    base_mask = (
        (Ma_r >= min_Ma_r) & (Ma_r <= max_Ma_r)
        & (d_au >= R_min_au) & (d_au <= R_max_au)
        & (Fa_over_Fk < fa_fk_max)
        & (mf_data >= mf_cut_lo) & (mf_data <= mf_cut_hi)
        & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() < 6)
        & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() > 1.0 / 6)
        & (df["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"].to_numpy() > 0.8)
    )


    # ── quantile-edge construction (use same quality gating) ───────────
    quantile_kw = {
        "Nx_bins"       : function_parameters["Nx_bins"],
        "Ny_bins"       : function_parameters["Ny_bins"],
        "log_x"         : function_parameters["log_x"],
        "poly_order"    : function_parameters["poly_order"],
        "auto_poly"     : function_parameters["auto_poly"],
        "max_poly_order": function_parameters["max_poly_order"],
        "model"         : model,
        "n_jobs"        : function_parameters["quantile_n_jobs"],
        "return_counts" : False,
        "return_avg_y"  : False,
        "low_pct"       : function_parameters["quantile_low_pct"],
        "high_pct"      : function_parameters["quantile_high_pct"],
    }

    qmask  = base_mask
    qfuncs = func.compute_quantile_edges_function(
        df["d"][qmask], df["Vsw"][qmask], **quantile_kw
    )

    if model == "parker":
        coefs   = func.build_parker_surrogates(
            qfuncs["fit_parameters"],
            r_min       = qfuncs["x_edges"][0],
            r_max       = qfuncs["x_edges"][-1],
            n_samples   = function_parameters["parker_n_samples"],
            poly_degree = function_parameters["parker_poly_degree"],
        )
        V_edges = func.eval_parker_surrogates(d_au, coefs)  # shape (E, N)
    elif model == "empirical":
        edge_funcs = qfuncs["edge_functions"]
        V_edges = np.vstack([
            (f(d_au) if f is not None else np.full(d_au.shape[0], np.nan, dtype=float))
            for f in edge_funcs
        ])
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown model '{model}'")

    if V_edges.ndim != 2 or V_edges.shape[0] < 2:
        raise RuntimeError("V_edges has invalid shape; need at least two edges (E>=2).")

    E = V_edges.shape[0]
    if len(bin_edges) == E:
        labels = [f"{round(bin_edges[j-1], 2)} – {round(bin_edges[j], 2)}" for j in range(1, E)]
    else:
        labels = [f"Bin {j}/{E-1}" for j in range(1, E)]

    binned_by_vals = df[binned_by].to_numpy()

    # ── per-bin worker ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _process_bin(jj):
        fin = {}

        low, high = V_edges[jj - 1], V_edges[jj]
        with np.errstate(invalid="ignore"):
            mask2 = base_mask & (binned_by_vals > low) & (binned_by_vals <= high)

        if not np.any(mask2):
            fin["label"] = labels[jj - 1]
            return jj - 1, fin

        sub = df.loc[mask2]

        # ---- Fa diagnostics vs R & Ma ---------------------------------
        # d_rsun = sub["d"].to_numpy() * AU_TO_R_SUN
        # Ma_sel = sub[what_Ma].to_numpy()
        # print('Got')
        # Fa_sel = Fa[mask2]
        # AW_tot = sub[which_AW_flux].to_numpy()

        # r_ok    = (d_rsun > 0)
        # r_okFa  = r_ok & (Fa_sel > 0)
        # r_okAW  = r_ok & (AW_tot > 0)

        # fin["Fa_R"]     = func.binned_quantity(d_rsun[r_okFa], Fa_sel[r_okFa], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["Fa_tot_R"] = func.binned_quantity(d_rsun[r_okAW], AW_tot[r_okAW], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ma_okFa = (Fa_sel > 0)
        # ma_okAW = (AW_tot > 0)

        # fin["Fa_Ma"]     = func.binned_quantity(Ma_sel[ma_okFa],  Fa_sel[ma_okFa],  what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["Fa_tot_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_sel[ma_okAW],  AW_tot[ma_okAW],  what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- CH09 cascade ---------------------------------------------
        # CH09 = turb.CH_09_cascade_rate(
        #     sub,
        #     col_Ma   = what_Ma,
        #     col_r_au = "d",
        #     col_V0   = "Vsw",
        #     col_VA0  = "Va",
        #     col_Zp2  = f"Zp_amp{use_perp}",
        #     col_rho  = "rho",
        #     units    = units,
        #     n_bins   = n_fit_bins,
        #     poly_deg = deg_CH09_Q,
        #     R_min    = R_min_au,
        #     R_max    = R_max_au,
        #     use_binning = True,
        #     hetero_fit  = "ols",
        #     quant_is_squared = True,
        # )
        # fin["CH09_fits"] = CH09["fits"]

        # x_CH09_au = np.asarray(CH09["x"])
        # y_CH09    = np.asarray(CH09["y"])
        # Ma_CH09   = np.asarray(CH09['Ma'])
        # sh_CH09   = np.abs(np.asarray(CH09["scale_height"]))

        # r_okQ   = (x_CH09_au > 0) & (y_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(x_CH09_au) & np.isfinite(y_CH09)
        # ma_okQ  = (Ma_CH09 > 0) & (Ma_CH09 <= max_Ma_r) & (y_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_CH09) & np.isfinite(y_CH09)
        # r_okSH  = (x_CH09_au > 0) & (sh_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(x_CH09_au) & np.isfinite(sh_CH09)
        # ma_okSH = (Ma_CH09 > 0) & (Ma_CH09 <= max_Ma_r) & (sh_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_CH09) & np.isfinite(sh_CH09)

        # fin["CH09_R"]               = func.binned_quantity(x_CH09_au[r_okQ] * AU_TO_R_SUN, y_CH09[r_okQ], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_Ma"]              = func.binned_quantity(Ma_CH09[ma_okQ],                y_CH09[ma_okQ], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_scale_height_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(x_CH09_au[r_okSH] * AU_TO_R_SUN, sh_CH09[r_okSH], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_scale_height_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_CH09[ma_okSH],               sh_CH09[ma_okSH], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- Cr09 cascade (ONLY mask Tp in-place; keep rows/order) -----
        idx_span_sub = span_ok[mask2]

        # backup Tp, set NaN only where span fails, call, then restore

        sub.loc[~idx_span_sub, which_Tp] = np.nan

        Cr09_dict, fits = turb.Cr09_cascade_rate(
            sub,
            deg_Tp  = deg_Cr09_Tp,
            deg_Te  = deg_Cr09_Te,
            deg_n   = deg_Cr09_Np,
            deg_q   = deg_Cr09_qe,
            deg_phi = deg_Cr09_Phi,
            n_bins  = n_fit_bins,
            R_min   = R_min_au,
            R_max   = R_max_au,
            which_Te= which_Te,
            which_Tp= which_Tp,
        )


        fin["Cr09_fits"] = fits

        d_cr_rsun = Cr09_dict["d"].to_numpy() * AU_TO_R_SUN
        Ma_cr     = Cr09_dict['Ma'].to_numpy()

        for Q in ("Qe", "Qp", "Qe_qpar"):
            y = Cr09_dict[Q].to_numpy()
            r_ok = (d_cr_rsun > 0) & (y > 0) & np.isfinite(d_cr_rsun) & np.isfinite(y)
            m_ok = (Ma_cr > 0) & (Ma_cr <= max_Ma_r) & (y > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_cr) & np.isfinite(y)
            fin[f"Cr09_{Q}_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(d_cr_rsun[r_ok], y[r_ok], what=what, bins=nbins)
            fin[f"Cr09_{Q}_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_cr[m_ok],    y[m_ok], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- PP98 (SPAN-quality only, same mask) -----------------------
        # if np.any(idx_span_sub):
        #     xv = AU_TO_R_SUN * sub["d"].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub]
        #     yv = 0.5 * (4.0 / 3.0) * sub["QPP98"].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub]
        #     fin["PP98_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(xv, yv, what=what, bins=nbins)
        #     fin["PP98_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(sub[what_Ma].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub], yv, what=what, bins=nbins)

        fin["label"] = labels[jj - 1]
        print(jj)
        return jj - 1, fin

    # ── parallel execution ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    results = Parallel(n_jobs=n_jobs)(delayed(_process_bin)(jj) for jj in range(1, E))

    out = {"bins": bin_edges}
    for idx, res in results:
        out[idx] = res
    return out


function_parameters = {

    "Max_Vx_angle"              : 160,
    "Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"    : 2.5,

              & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() < function_parameters["Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"])
              & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() > 1.0 / function_parameters["Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"])
              & (df["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"].to_numpy() > function_parameters["Min_SPAN_SPAN_media_ratio"])


              )

    base_mask = (
        (Ma_r >= min_Ma_r) & (Ma_r <= max_Ma_r)
        & (d_au >= R_min_au) & (d_au <= R_max_au)
        & (Fa_over_Fk < fa_fk_max)
        & (mf_data >= mf_cut_lo) & (mf_data <= mf_cut_hi)
        & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() < 6)
        & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() > 1.0 / 6)
        & (df["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"].to_numpy() > 0.8)
    )


    # ── quantile-edge construction (use same quality gating) ───────────
    quantile_kw = {
        "Nx_bins"       : function_parameters["Nx_bins"],
        "Ny_bins"       : function_parameters["Ny_bins"],
        "log_x"         : function_parameters["log_x"],
        "poly_order"    : function_parameters["poly_order"],
        "auto_poly"     : function_parameters["auto_poly"],
        "max_poly_order": function_parameters["max_poly_order"],
        "model"         : model,
        "n_jobs"        : function_parameters["quantile_n_jobs"],
        "return_counts" : False,
        "return_avg_y"  : False,
        "low_pct"       : function_parameters["quantile_low_pct"],
        "high_pct"      : function_parameters["quantile_high_pct"],
    }

    qmask  = base_mask
    qfuncs = func.compute_quantile_edges_function(
        df["d"][qmask], df["Vsw"][qmask], **quantile_kw
    )

    if model == "parker":
        coefs   = func.build_parker_surrogates(
            qfuncs["fit_parameters"],
            r_min       = qfuncs["x_edges"][0],
            r_max       = qfuncs["x_edges"][-1],
            n_samples   = function_parameters["parker_n_samples"],
            poly_degree = function_parameters["parker_poly_degree"],
        )
        V_edges = func.eval_parker_surrogates(d_au, coefs)  # shape (E, N)
    elif model == "empirical":
        edge_funcs = qfuncs["edge_functions"]
        V_edges = np.vstack([
            (f(d_au) if f is not None else np.full(d_au.shape[0], np.nan, dtype=float))
            for f in edge_funcs
        ])
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown model '{model}'")

    if V_edges.ndim != 2 or V_edges.shape[0] < 2:
        raise RuntimeError("V_edges has invalid shape; need at least two edges (E>=2).")

    E = V_edges.shape[0]
    if len(bin_edges) == E:
        labels = [f"{round(bin_edges[j-1], 2)} – {round(bin_edges[j], 2)}" for j in range(1, E)]
    else:
        labels = [f"Bin {j}/{E-1}" for j in range(1, E)]

    binned_by_vals = df[binned_by].to_numpy()

    # ── per-bin worker ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _process_bin(jj):
        fin = {}

        low, high = V_edges[jj - 1], V_edges[jj]
        with np.errstate(invalid="ignore"):
            mask2 = base_mask & (binned_by_vals > low) & (binned_by_vals <= high)

        if not np.any(mask2):
            fin["label"] = labels[jj - 1]
            return jj - 1, fin

        sub = df.loc[mask2]

        # ---- Fa diagnostics vs R & Ma ---------------------------------
        # d_rsun = sub["d"].to_numpy() * AU_TO_R_SUN
        # Ma_sel = sub[what_Ma].to_numpy()
        # print('Got')
        # Fa_sel = Fa[mask2]
        # AW_tot = sub[which_AW_flux].to_numpy()

        # r_ok    = (d_rsun > 0)
        # r_okFa  = r_ok & (Fa_sel > 0)
        # r_okAW  = r_ok & (AW_tot > 0)

        # fin["Fa_R"]     = func.binned_quantity(d_rsun[r_okFa], Fa_sel[r_okFa], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["Fa_tot_R"] = func.binned_quantity(d_rsun[r_okAW], AW_tot[r_okAW], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ma_okFa = (Fa_sel > 0)
        # ma_okAW = (AW_tot > 0)

        # fin["Fa_Ma"]     = func.binned_quantity(Ma_sel[ma_okFa],  Fa_sel[ma_okFa],  what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["Fa_tot_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_sel[ma_okAW],  AW_tot[ma_okAW],  what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- CH09 cascade ---------------------------------------------
        # CH09 = turb.CH_09_cascade_rate(
        #     sub,
        #     col_Ma   = what_Ma,
        #     col_r_au = "d",
        #     col_V0   = "Vsw",
        #     col_VA0  = "Va",
        #     col_Zp2  = f"Zp_amp{use_perp}",
        #     col_rho  = "rho",
        #     units    = units,
        #     n_bins   = n_fit_bins,
        #     poly_deg = deg_CH09_Q,
        #     R_min    = R_min_au,
        #     R_max    = R_max_au,
        #     use_binning = True,
        #     hetero_fit  = "ols",
        #     quant_is_squared = True,
        # )
        # fin["CH09_fits"] = CH09["fits"]

        # x_CH09_au = np.asarray(CH09["x"])
        # y_CH09    = np.asarray(CH09["y"])
        # Ma_CH09   = np.asarray(CH09['Ma'])
        # sh_CH09   = np.abs(np.asarray(CH09["scale_height"]))

        # r_okQ   = (x_CH09_au > 0) & (y_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(x_CH09_au) & np.isfinite(y_CH09)
        # ma_okQ  = (Ma_CH09 > 0) & (Ma_CH09 <= max_Ma_r) & (y_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_CH09) & np.isfinite(y_CH09)
        # r_okSH  = (x_CH09_au > 0) & (sh_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(x_CH09_au) & np.isfinite(sh_CH09)
        # ma_okSH = (Ma_CH09 > 0) & (Ma_CH09 <= max_Ma_r) & (sh_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_CH09) & np.isfinite(sh_CH09)

        # fin["CH09_R"]               = func.binned_quantity(x_CH09_au[r_okQ] * AU_TO_R_SUN, y_CH09[r_okQ], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_Ma"]              = func.binned_quantity(Ma_CH09[ma_okQ],                y_CH09[ma_okQ], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_scale_height_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(x_CH09_au[r_okSH] * AU_TO_R_SUN, sh_CH09[r_okSH], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_scale_height_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_CH09[ma_okSH],               sh_CH09[ma_okSH], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- Cr09 cascade (ONLY mask Tp in-place; keep rows/order) -----
        idx_span_sub = span_ok[mask2]

        # backup Tp, set NaN only where span fails, call, then restore

        sub.loc[~idx_span_sub, which_Tp] = np.nan

        Cr09_dict, fits = turb.Cr09_cascade_rate(
            sub,
            deg_Tp  = deg_Cr09_Tp,
            deg_Te  = deg_Cr09_Te,
            deg_n   = deg_Cr09_Np,
            deg_q   = deg_Cr09_qe,
            deg_phi = deg_Cr09_Phi,
            n_bins  = n_fit_bins,
            R_min   = R_min_au,
            R_max   = R_max_au,
            which_Te= which_Te,
            which_Tp= which_Tp,
        )


        fin["Cr09_fits"] = fits

        d_cr_rsun = Cr09_dict["d"].to_numpy() * AU_TO_R_SUN
        Ma_cr     = Cr09_dict['Ma'].to_numpy()

        for Q in ("Qe", "Qp", "Qe_qpar"):
            y = Cr09_dict[Q].to_numpy()
            r_ok = (d_cr_rsun > 0) & (y > 0) & np.isfinite(d_cr_rsun) & np.isfinite(y)
            m_ok = (Ma_cr > 0) & (Ma_cr <= max_Ma_r) & (y > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_cr) & np.isfinite(y)
            fin[f"Cr09_{Q}_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(d_cr_rsun[r_ok], y[r_ok], what=what, bins=nbins)
            fin[f"Cr09_{Q}_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_cr[m_ok],    y[m_ok], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- PP98 (SPAN-quality only, same mask) -----------------------
        # if np.any(idx_span_sub):
        #     xv = AU_TO_R_SUN * sub["d"].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub]
        #     yv = 0.5 * (4.0 / 3.0) * sub["QPP98"].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub]
        #     fin["PP98_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(xv, yv, what=what, bins=nbins)
        #     fin["PP98_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(sub[what_Ma].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub], yv, what=what, bins=nbins)

        fin["label"] = labels[jj - 1]
        print(jj)
        return jj - 1, fin

    # ── parallel execution ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    results = Parallel(n_jobs=n_jobs)(delayed(_process_bin)(jj) for jj in range(1, E))

    out = {"bins": bin_edges}
    for idx, res in results:
        out[idx] = res
    return out


function_parameters = {

    "Max_Vx_angle"              : 160,
    "Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"    : 2.5,
    "Min_SPAN_SPAN_media_ratio" : 0.85, 
    # compulsory data
    #'which_AW_flux'    : which_AW_flux,
    "merged_df"        : merged_df[::100],
    "which_Te"         : 'Te_spane',
    "which_Tp"         : 'T_p_Davin',

    # binning & selection
    "binned_by"        : "Vsw",
    "bin_edges"        : [0, 250, 350, 450, 800],
    "percentiles"      : [0.,0.4,0.4, 1 , 0, 0],

    "fa_fk_max_ratio"  : 1e2,
    "mf_min_percentile": 0.3,

    # modelling options
    "model"            : "parker",      # {"empirical", "parker"}
    "n_fit_bins"       :  1000,
    "statistic"        : "mean",
    "units"            : "SI",

    # Parker‑surrogate hyper‑parameters
    "parker_n_samples"  : 1000,
    "parker_poly_degree": 10,

    # quantile‑grid hyper‑parameters
    "Nx_bins"          : 60,
    "Ny_bins"          : 4,
    "log_x"            : True,
    "poly_order"       : None,
    "auto_poly"        : True,
    "max_poly_order"   : 4,
    "quantile_n_jobs"  : 1,
    "quantile_low_pct" : 0.01,
    "quantile_high_pct": 97.0,

    # miscellaneous numerics
    "nbins"            : 120,
    "what"             : "mean",
    "n_jobs"           : 4,     # joblib cores
    "R_min"            : 9/215.032,
    "R_max"            : 100/215.032,
    "max_Ma_r"         : 30,
    "min_Ma_r"         : 0.3,

    # constants
    "AU_TO_R_SUN"      : 215.032,   
    "R_units"          : 'AU',



    # Polynomial order
    'pol_order'       : {
                                'CHO9': {  'Q'  : 2},

                                'CrO9': { 'Tp'  : 2, 
                                          'Te'  : 2,
                                          'Np'  : 2,
                                          'Ne'  : 2,
                                          'qe'  : 2,
                                          'Phi' : 6} }}





final_results = run_parallel(function_parameters)





save_path                         = '/Users/turbulator/work/turb_amplitudes/heating_rates/data/'

# Session 3100, line 31
import numpy as np
from joblib import Parallel, delayed
from astropy import units as u, constants as const


def run_parallel(function_parameters):
    """
    Parallel-compute heating-rate diagnostics across user-defined bins.

    External deps expected in scope: func, turb, ampl_funcs, u, const.
    """

    # ── unpack essentials ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    df          = function_parameters["merged_df"]
    bin_edges   = np.asarray(function_parameters["bin_edges"])
    binned_by   = function_parameters["binned_by"]
    percentiles = np.asarray(function_parameters["percentiles"])

    which_Tp    = function_parameters["which_Tp"]
    which_Te    = function_parameters["which_Te"]
    n_fit_bins  = function_parameters["n_fit_bins"]
    nbins       = function_parameters["nbins"]
    what        = function_parameters["what"]
    units       = function_parameters["units"]
    n_jobs      = function_parameters["n_jobs"]

    AU_TO_R_SUN = function_parameters["AU_TO_R_SUN"]
    AU_IN_KM    = function_parameters.get("AU_IN_KM", 149_597_870.7)
    R_units     = function_parameters.get("R_units", "AU")

    R_min_user  = function_parameters["R_min"]
    R_max_user  = function_parameters["R_max"]
    if R_units.lower() in {"rsun", "rs", "r_sun", "r⊙"}:
        R_min_au, R_max_au = R_min_user / AU_TO_R_SUN, R_max_user / AU_TO_R_SUN
        R_min_rsun, R_max_rsun = R_min_user, R_max_user
    else:
        R_min_au, R_max_au = R_min_user, R_max_user
        R_min_rsun, R_max_rsun = R_min_au * AU_TO_R_SUN, R_max_au * AU_TO_R_SUN

    max_Ma_r    = function_parameters["max_Ma_r"]
    min_Ma_r    = function_parameters["min_Ma_r"]
    fa_fk_max   = function_parameters["fa_fk_max_ratio"]
    mf_min_pct  = function_parameters["mf_min_percentile"]
    model       = function_parameters["model"]

    #which_AW_flux = function_parameters["which_AW_flux"]
    use_perp      = function_parameters.get("use_perp", "")

    # Allow both CH09/Cr09 and the common O/0 typos.
    def _get_pol(section, key):
        pol = function_parameters["pol_order"]
        for s in {section, section.replace("0", "O"), section.replace("O", "0")}:
            if s in pol and key in pol[s]:
                return pol[s][key]
        raise KeyError(f"pol_order['{section}']['{key}'] not found (tried O/0 variants).")

    deg_CH09_Q  = _get_pol("CH09", "Q")
    deg_Cr09_Tp = _get_pol("Cr09", "Tp")
    deg_Cr09_Te = _get_pol("Cr09", "Te")
    deg_Cr09_Np = _get_pol("Cr09", "Np")
    deg_Cr09_qe = _get_pol("Cr09", "qe")
    deg_Cr09_Phi= _get_pol("Cr09", "Phi")

    # ── numeric arrays (no unit-ed Series) ─────────────────────────────
    d_au   = df["d"].to_numpy()
    Vsw    = df["Vsw"].to_numpy()
    rho    = df["rho"].to_numpy()      # kg/m^3 downstream
    Ma_r   = df[what_Ma].to_numpy()
    Va     = df["Va"].to_numpy()
    Zp_amp = df[f"Zp_amp{use_perp}"].to_numpy()
    Zm_amp = df[f"Zm_amp{use_perp}"].to_numpy()

    # ── mass flux proxy & stable cuts ───────────────────────────────────
    mf_data = Vsw * (rho * 1e9) * ((d_au * AU_IN_KM) ** 2)
    mf_cut_lo = np.percentile(mf_data[np.isfinite(mf_data)], mf_min_pct)
    mf_cut_hi = np.percentile(mf_data[np.isfinite(mf_data)], 99.9)

    # ── Fa/Fk robustly (guard Fk≈0/NaN) ────────────────────────────────
    m_p = const.m_p.value
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        Fa, Fk = ampl_funcs.compute_fluxes(rho / m_p, Zp_amp, Zm_amp, Vsw, Va)
        Fa_over_Fk = Fa / Fk

    # ── data quality masks ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    span_ok = ((df["Vyx_angle"].to_numpy() < function_parameters["Max_Vx_angle"]) 

              & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() < function_parameters["Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"])
              & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() > 1.0 / function_parameters["Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"])
              & (df["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"].to_numpy() > function_parameters["Min_SPAN_SPAN_media_ratio"])


              )

    base_mask = (
        (Ma_r >= min_Ma_r) & (Ma_r <= max_Ma_r)
        & (d_au >= R_min_au) & (d_au <= R_max_au)
        & (Fa_over_Fk < fa_fk_max)
        & (mf_data >= mf_cut_lo) & (mf_data <= mf_cut_hi)
        & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() < 6)
        & (df["SPAN_QTN_ratio"].to_numpy() > 1.0 / 6)
        & (df["SWEAP_t2t_med_ratio"].to_numpy() > 0.8)
    )


    # ── quantile-edge construction (use same quality gating) ───────────
    quantile_kw = {
        "Nx_bins"       : function_parameters["Nx_bins"],
        "Ny_bins"       : function_parameters["Ny_bins"],
        "log_x"         : function_parameters["log_x"],
        "poly_order"    : function_parameters["poly_order"],
        "auto_poly"     : function_parameters["auto_poly"],
        "max_poly_order": function_parameters["max_poly_order"],
        "model"         : model,
        "n_jobs"        : function_parameters["quantile_n_jobs"],
        "return_counts" : False,
        "return_avg_y"  : False,
        "low_pct"       : function_parameters["quantile_low_pct"],
        "high_pct"      : function_parameters["quantile_high_pct"],
    }

    qmask  = base_mask
    qfuncs = func.compute_quantile_edges_function(
        df["d"][qmask], df["Vsw"][qmask], **quantile_kw
    )

    if model == "parker":
        coefs   = func.build_parker_surrogates(
            qfuncs["fit_parameters"],
            r_min       = qfuncs["x_edges"][0],
            r_max       = qfuncs["x_edges"][-1],
            n_samples   = function_parameters["parker_n_samples"],
            poly_degree = function_parameters["parker_poly_degree"],
        )
        V_edges = func.eval_parker_surrogates(d_au, coefs)  # shape (E, N)
    elif model == "empirical":
        edge_funcs = qfuncs["edge_functions"]
        V_edges = np.vstack([
            (f(d_au) if f is not None else np.full(d_au.shape[0], np.nan, dtype=float))
            for f in edge_funcs
        ])
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown model '{model}'")

    if V_edges.ndim != 2 or V_edges.shape[0] < 2:
        raise RuntimeError("V_edges has invalid shape; need at least two edges (E>=2).")

    E = V_edges.shape[0]
    if len(bin_edges) == E:
        labels = [f"{round(bin_edges[j-1], 2)} – {round(bin_edges[j], 2)}" for j in range(1, E)]
    else:
        labels = [f"Bin {j}/{E-1}" for j in range(1, E)]

    binned_by_vals = df[binned_by].to_numpy()

    # ── per-bin worker ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _process_bin(jj):
        fin = {}

        low, high = V_edges[jj - 1], V_edges[jj]
        with np.errstate(invalid="ignore"):
            mask2 = base_mask & (binned_by_vals > low) & (binned_by_vals <= high)

        if not np.any(mask2):
            fin["label"] = labels[jj - 1]
            return jj - 1, fin

        sub = df.loc[mask2]

        # ---- Fa diagnostics vs R & Ma ---------------------------------
        # d_rsun = sub["d"].to_numpy() * AU_TO_R_SUN
        # Ma_sel = sub[what_Ma].to_numpy()
        # print('Got')
        # Fa_sel = Fa[mask2]
        # AW_tot = sub[which_AW_flux].to_numpy()

        # r_ok    = (d_rsun > 0)
        # r_okFa  = r_ok & (Fa_sel > 0)
        # r_okAW  = r_ok & (AW_tot > 0)

        # fin["Fa_R"]     = func.binned_quantity(d_rsun[r_okFa], Fa_sel[r_okFa], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["Fa_tot_R"] = func.binned_quantity(d_rsun[r_okAW], AW_tot[r_okAW], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ma_okFa = (Fa_sel > 0)
        # ma_okAW = (AW_tot > 0)

        # fin["Fa_Ma"]     = func.binned_quantity(Ma_sel[ma_okFa],  Fa_sel[ma_okFa],  what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["Fa_tot_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_sel[ma_okAW],  AW_tot[ma_okAW],  what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- CH09 cascade ---------------------------------------------
        # CH09 = turb.CH_09_cascade_rate(
        #     sub,
        #     col_Ma   = what_Ma,
        #     col_r_au = "d",
        #     col_V0   = "Vsw",
        #     col_VA0  = "Va",
        #     col_Zp2  = f"Zp_amp{use_perp}",
        #     col_rho  = "rho",
        #     units    = units,
        #     n_bins   = n_fit_bins,
        #     poly_deg = deg_CH09_Q,
        #     R_min    = R_min_au,
        #     R_max    = R_max_au,
        #     use_binning = True,
        #     hetero_fit  = "ols",
        #     quant_is_squared = True,
        # )
        # fin["CH09_fits"] = CH09["fits"]

        # x_CH09_au = np.asarray(CH09["x"])
        # y_CH09    = np.asarray(CH09["y"])
        # Ma_CH09   = np.asarray(CH09['Ma'])
        # sh_CH09   = np.abs(np.asarray(CH09["scale_height"]))

        # r_okQ   = (x_CH09_au > 0) & (y_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(x_CH09_au) & np.isfinite(y_CH09)
        # ma_okQ  = (Ma_CH09 > 0) & (Ma_CH09 <= max_Ma_r) & (y_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_CH09) & np.isfinite(y_CH09)
        # r_okSH  = (x_CH09_au > 0) & (sh_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(x_CH09_au) & np.isfinite(sh_CH09)
        # ma_okSH = (Ma_CH09 > 0) & (Ma_CH09 <= max_Ma_r) & (sh_CH09 > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_CH09) & np.isfinite(sh_CH09)

        # fin["CH09_R"]               = func.binned_quantity(x_CH09_au[r_okQ] * AU_TO_R_SUN, y_CH09[r_okQ], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_Ma"]              = func.binned_quantity(Ma_CH09[ma_okQ],                y_CH09[ma_okQ], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_scale_height_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(x_CH09_au[r_okSH] * AU_TO_R_SUN, sh_CH09[r_okSH], what=what, bins=nbins)
        # fin["CH09_scale_height_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_CH09[ma_okSH],               sh_CH09[ma_okSH], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- Cr09 cascade (ONLY mask Tp in-place; keep rows/order) -----

        # backup Tp, set NaN only where span fails, call, then restore

        sub.loc[~idx_span_sub, which_Tp] = np.nan

        Cr09_dict, fits = turb.Cr09_cascade_rate(

            deg_Tp  = deg_Cr09_Tp,
            deg_Te  = deg_Cr09_Te,
            deg_n   = deg_Cr09_Np,
            deg_q   = deg_Cr09_qe,
            deg_phi = deg_Cr09_Phi,
            n_bins  = n_fit_bins,
            R_min   = R_min_au,
            R_max   = R_max_au,
            which_Te= which_Te,
            which_Tp= which_Tp,
        )


        fin["Cr09_fits"] = fits

        d_cr_rsun = Cr09_dict["d"].to_numpy() * AU_TO_R_SUN
        Ma_cr     = Cr09_dict['Ma'].to_numpy()

        for Q in ("Qe", "Qp", "Qe_qpar"):
            y = Cr09_dict[Q].to_numpy()
            r_ok = (d_cr_rsun > 0) & (y > 0) & np.isfinite(d_cr_rsun) & np.isfinite(y)
            m_ok = (Ma_cr > 0) & (Ma_cr <= max_Ma_r) & (y > 0) & np.isfinite(Ma_cr) & np.isfinite(y)
            fin[f"Cr09_{Q}_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(d_cr_rsun[r_ok], y[r_ok], what=what, bins=nbins)
            fin[f"Cr09_{Q}_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(Ma_cr[m_ok],    y[m_ok], what=what, bins=nbins)

        # ---- PP98 (SPAN-quality only, same mask) -----------------------
        # if np.any(idx_span_sub):
        #     xv = AU_TO_R_SUN * sub["d"].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub]
        #     yv = 0.5 * (4.0 / 3.0) * sub["QPP98"].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub]
        #     fin["PP98_R"]  = func.binned_quantity(xv, yv, what=what, bins=nbins)
        #     fin["PP98_Ma"] = func.binned_quantity(sub[what_Ma].to_numpy()[idx_span_sub], yv, what=what, bins=nbins)

        fin["label"] = labels[jj - 1]
        print(jj)
        return jj - 1, fin

    # ── parallel execution ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    results = Parallel(n_jobs=n_jobs)(delayed(_process_bin)(jj) for jj in range(1, E))

    out = {"bins": bin_edges}
    for idx, res in results:
        out[idx] = res
    return out


function_parameters = {

    "Max_Vx_angle"              : 160,
    "Max_Min_SPAN_QTN_ratio"    : 2.5,
    "Min_SPAN_SPAN_media_ratio" : 0.85, 
    # compulsory data
    #'which_AW_flux'    : which_AW_flux,
    "merged_df"        : merged_df[::100],
    "which_Te"         : 'Te_spane',

    # binning & selection
    "binned_by"        : "Vsw",
    "bin_edges"        : [0, 250, 350, 450, 800],
    "percentiles"      : [0.,0.4,0.4, 1 , 0, 0],

    "fa_fk_max_ratio"  : 1e2,
    "mf_min_percentile": 0.3,

    # modelling options
    "model"            : "parker",      # {"empirical", "parker"}

    "statistic"        : "mean",
    "units"            : "SI",

    # Parker‑surrogate hyper‑parameters
    "parker_n_samples"  : 1000,
    "parker_poly_degree": 10,

    # quantile‑grid hyper‑parameters
    "Nx_bins"          : 60,
    "Ny_bins"          : 4,
    "log_x"            : True,
    "poly_order"       : None,

    "max_poly_order"   : 4,
    "quantile_n_jobs"  : 1,
    "quantile_low_pct" : 0.01,
    "quantile_high_pct": 97.0,

    # miscellaneous numerics
    "nbins"            : 120,

    "R_min"            : 9/215.032,
    "R_max"            : 100/215.032,
    "max_Ma_r"         : 30,
    "min_Ma_r"         : 0.3,

    # constants
    "AU_TO_R_SUN"      : 215.032,   
    "R_units"          : 'AU',



    # Polynomial order
    'pol_order'       : {
                                'CHO9': {  'Q'  : 2},

                                'CrO9': { 'Tp'  : 2, 
                                          'Te'  : 2,

# Session 3100, line 33
import sqlite3
import os

# Path to IPython history DB
hist_file = os.path.expanduser("~/.ipython/profile_default/history.sqlite")

if not os.path.exists(hist_file):
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"History DB not found at {hist_file}")

# Connect to the history database
conn = sqlite3.connect(hist_file)
cur = conn.cursor()

# Query last 200 entries (order by session, then line)
cur.execute("""
SELECT session, line, source 
FROM history 
ORDER BY session DESC, line DESC 
LIMIT 200
""")
rows = cur.fetchall()
conn.close()

# Reverse so newest is last
rows.reverse()

# Save to local file
out_path = "last_200_runs.txt"
with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    for session, line, source in rows:
        f.write(f"# Session {session}, line {line}\n{source}\n\n")

print(f"Saved last 200 inputs to {out_path}")
